- **p-code-review-correctness**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the correctness / code-quality channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Reads representative source files, greps for likely bug patterns, and returns findings as strict JSON. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead, which orchestrates this agent alongside the security and reuse channels. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-correctness.md))
- **p-code-review-reuse**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the reuse / duplication channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Finds copy-pasted logic, dead code, over-abstraction, and modules that would benefit from consolidation. Returns strict JSON. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead, which orchestrates this agent alongside the correctness and security channels. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-reuse.md))
- **p-code-review-security**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the security channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Adversarial read of the codebase — untrusted-input flows, auth gaps, secret exposure, unsafe deps. Returns strict JSON. Inherits the model selected in the invoking Claude session. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-security.md))

([ovsw/maplewoodyearround.com-2026](https://github.com/ovsw/maplewoodyearround.com-2026/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/pages.md))
- **pages-audit**: Cross-section consistency auditor — compares the project's primary multi-section surface (tab bar / dashboard sections / top-level views) side-by-side to find consistency drift no single-screen review catches: header pattern, section-title typography, card/row pattern, empty state, CTA hit-size, surface treatment. Majority-rules. Read-only; produces a deviation report with property / majority / deviator / fix. NOT a single-screen quality grader. ([vindm/dotclaude](https://github.com/vindm/dotclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/design/agents/pages-audit.md) · ★1)
- **pages-partenaires**: Crée les deux pages partenaires de Next Impact : une page APPORTEURS D'AFFAIRES (freelances/consultants qui recommandent contre commission) et une page AGENCES & STUDIOS (qui sous-traitent le dev en marque blanche). Deux pages distinctes, deux audiences, deux peurs différentes. Utilise cet agent quand on demande de créer, modifier ou débuguer la page apporteurs, la page sous-traitance/agences, le programme de partenariat, l'apport d'affaires, la marque blanche, ou les CTA partenaires. Il retourne du code Next.js (App Router) prêt à intégrer + un résumé des fichiers créés/modifiés. Use for Discord communication, Linear task management, persistent markdown memory, and read-only research via WebSearch/WebFetch and a headless Playwright browser. ([kylep/multi](https://github.com/kylep/multi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pai.md) · ★3)
- **pai-recaller**: Active-memory sub-agent for Pai. ([kylep/multi](https://github.com/kylep/multi/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pai-self-improver.md) · ★3)

- **pair-red-contract-verifier**: Independent validator of a Pair acceptance contract, and its sealer. Reproduces every witness and control against the unfixed base, re-derives the inventory from the authoritative producer, emits every gap it finds in one typed rejection — and, when the contract holds, runs the deterministic sealer script in the same execution and returns the snapshot. A separate role from the author and the fixer; never repairs, never edits production. ([foomakers/pair](https://github.com/foomakers/pair/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-red-contract-verifier.md) · ★9 · 7 copies)
- **pair-reviewer**: Independent final verifier of a single Pair PR head — custody by script (red-snapshot.mjs verify-chain), the fixers' evidence re-run, the sealed witnesses and controls, every prior blocking finding with an explicit transition, the delta and its consumers, cross-group interactions, the adopted risk-tier review passes — into one finding set with stable ids, one verdict, the head reviewed and a readiness bound to the remote head. Publishes the one first review or the one synthesis idempotently. Reviews only from the story, the PR, the code and the cycle's evidence — never the author's checkpoint… ([foomakers/pair](https://github.com/foomakers/pair/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-reviewer.md) · ★9 · 7 copies)

- **pal-review-ui**: Reviews the phase's screens for design-system use and accessibility (contrast, screen-reader labels). The phase skill dispatches it only when screens changed. - **pal-reviewer**: PAL codereview/precommit subagent — uses the pal MCP codereview and precommit tools for multi-model validation before commits. Spawned by Gandalf alongside quest teammates. Reports issues to the lead via SendMessage. ([justinjdev/fellowship](https://github.com/justinjdev/fellowship/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/palantir.md) · ★5)
- **palantir**: Researches a question on the open web (an advisory, a claim to fact-check, practice beyond the training cutoff). Not for auditing this repo's code, not for a library's own docs (context7). ([techmefr/mentis](https://github.com/techmefr/mentis/blob/HEAD/agents/palantir.md))
- **Palantir-Agent-Stack-Sessions**: Agent Stack은 한 번의 chat completion보다 오래 지속되는 업무를 session, typed context items와 event/state machine으로 연결하는 기반이다. 여러 참여자가 다른 앱에서 같은 task에 기여하고 agent가 검토를 기다렸다가 입력이 오면 이어갈 수 있다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Agent-Stack-Sessions.md))
- **Palantir-AI-FDE-and-Skills**: AI FDE는 Foundry를 대화형으로 조작하는 개발 agent다. 별도 service account가 아니라 현재 사용자의 session과 권한으로 실행하며 AIP enablement가 필요하다. Ontology 변경에는 Global Branching enablement가 권장된다. 코드를 async function으로 배포하는 [[Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents]]와 실행 모델이 다르다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-AI-FDE-and-Skills.md))
- **Palantir-Assist-Content-and-Context**: AIP Assist는 플랫폼 사용을 지원하는 대화형 도구다. Default는 platform/developer/custom source를 고르고, 전용 documentation mode와 custom chatbot을 선택할 수 있다. AIP enablement와 해당 기능의 enrollment availability를 확인한다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Assist-Content-and-Context.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Application-State**: Model context, tool 입력/출력과 UI state를 각각 정한다. LLM이 대답한 사실과 필요한 variable이 갱신된 사실은 다르다. 2025년 4월 개편 이력과 2026년 10월 전용 문서의 계약을 함께 구분한다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Application-State.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Retrieval-and-Citations**: Chatbot Studio는 이전 Agent Studio 명칭의 interactive assistant 구성 도구다. Instructions, tool/variable description은 compiled system prompt를 만든다. LLM에 주는 context와 실행 가능한 tool을 별도로 설계한다. Model은 enrollment에서 활성화한 일부만 선택할 수 있다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Retrieval-and-Citations.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Sessions-and-Distribution**: Interactive chatbot을 Function, API, Workshop 또는 Marketplace로 배포할 때 같은 prompt가 같은 실행 환경을 보장하지 않는다. Saved/published version, resource dependency, session input과 client tool 지원을 함께 고정한다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Sessions-and-Distribution.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Tools-and-Commands**: Chatbot tool은 LLM이 control flow를 정하고 입력값을 구성해 호출하는 경로다. 다만 Action과 Function은 application variable의 deterministic input(string과 object set 입력만)을, Command는 beta configuration에서 preset 값이나 string/object set application variable을 쓰도록 입력을 고정할 수 있다. Action은 Ontology edit, Object query는 허용한 type/property의 filter/aggregation/inspection/link traversal, Function은 published Logic을 포함한 함수 호출을 담당한다. Update app ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Tools-and-Commands.md))
- **Palantir-Foundry-Agents**: - [[Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents|함수로 배포하는 Pro-code agent]] - [[Palantir-AI-FDE-and-Skills|대화형 AI FDE와 재사용 지침]] - [[Palantir-Agent-Stack-Sessions|Agent Stack의 장기 session과 event]] - [[Palantir-Chatbot-Application-State|Chatbot의 context, 변수와 UI trigger]] - [[Palantir-Chatbot-Retrieval-and-Citations|Retrieval과 citation 계약]] - [[Palantir-Chatbot-Tools-and-Commands|Tool mode와 client command]] - [[Palantir-C ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Foundry-Agents.md))
- **Palantir-Generated-Applications-and-Seed-Data**: Pilot은 요구에서 Ontology, design specification과 React OSDK 앱을 만드는 개발 workflow다. 2026년 3월 beta 도입 계약이며 생성된 앱의 업무 정확성, 인가와 배포 성공을 직접 시험한 기록은 아니다. Pilot은 2026-10-29 GA로 발표됐으며 현재 문서 기준의 발행 절차는 [[Palantir-Pilot-Application-and-Widget-Release|Pilot 앱과 위젯의 발행 계약]]에서 다룬다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Generated-Applications-and-Seed-Data.md))
- **Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents**: Pro-code agent는 Code Repository의 custom agent logic을 asynchronous Foundry function으로 배포하는 경로다. Claude Agent SDK, OpenAI Agents SDK와 Google ADK template을 제공하며 OSDK, Ontology MCP와 Palantir MCP를 사용한다. 확인일 기준 beta이며 실제 agent 생성, 권한과 장애를 시험한 기록은 아니다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents.md))
- **palantir-stone**: The palantír (seeing-stone) — the ONLY role that WRITES to external systems via session MCP tools (currently Asana: update tasks, add comments, create tasks). Use ONLY with a dispatch that enumerates every literal write on a known target per `agent_doc/palantir-protocol.md`; all judgment about the values happens upstream. Never deletes; creation is limited to tasks. For reads use `mirror-of-galadriel`. ([twjohnwu/tlor-orchestration](https://github.com/twjohnwu/tlor-orchestration/blob/HEAD/agents/palantir-stone.md))

- **pan-conductor**: Hierarchical orchestrator for /pan:exec-phase --hierarchical. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-conductor.md))
- **pan-counterfactual**: Explores a phase's alternative scenario in an isolated git worktree, compares against the original plan, and produces a structured report. Destructive-operation gated. - **pan-debugger**: Investigates bugs using scientific method, manages debug sessions, handles checkpoints. Spawned by /pan:debug orchestrator. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-debugger.md))
- **pan-design-checker**: Independently verifies a design artifact (architecture, ADR, threat model, success criteria) BEFORE it is planned or implemented. Goal-backward, adversarial verification. Spawned by /pan:design-phase and /pan:focus-design. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-design-checker.md))
- **pan-designer**: Designs a roadmap phase before it is planned — architecture, decisions, phase-scoped ADR, threat-lite, and machine-checkable success criteria. Produces {phase}-design.md. - **pan-distiller**: AI code-bloat detection and rewrite agent. Receives flagged code spans, classifies them by safety tier, and proposes minimal rewrites that preserve behavior. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-distiller.md))
- **pan-document_code**: Explores codebase and writes structured analysis documents. Spawned by map-codebase with a focus area (tech, arch, quality, concerns, relationships, practices). Writes documents directly to reduce orchestrator context load. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-document_code.md))
- **pan-executor**: Executes PAN plans with atomic commits, deviation handling, checkpoint protocols, and state management. Spawned by the /pan:exec-phase orchestrator, /pan:quick and /pan:army. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-executor.md))
- **pan-experiment-runner**: Drives an external AI coding session against an experiment folder. Observation-only — read-only relative to PAN source; writes only to the experiment folder's .planning/. Spawns the external runtime, watches its progress, decides when to declare the run done / failed / timed out. Used by the v3.7.0 self-improvement loop. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-experiment-runner.md))
- **pan-hardener**: Security audit agent — OWASP Top 10 + STRIDE threat modeling across files changed in a phase. Read-only on source; writes only its report. Spawned by /pan:review-deep. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-hardener.md))
- **pan-integration-checker**: Verifies cross-phase integration and E2E flows. Checks that phases connect properly and user workflows complete end-to-end. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-integration-checker.md))
- **pan-knowledge**: Knowledge agent for grounded Q&A, multi-turn discussion, and playbook generation. Single agent, three modes (ask/discuss/playbook). Spawned by /pan:knowledge. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-knowledge.md))
- **pan-merge-agent**: Merge specialist — rebases an approved PR onto main, resolves conflicts, runs verification, and squash-merges via gh CLI. ([eltmon/overdeck](https://github.com/eltmon/overdeck/blob/HEAD/sync-sources/agents/pan-merge-agent.md) · ★36)
- **pan-meta-reviewer**: Reviews the reviewer + hardener output. Flags things both missed, disputes findings that look overstated, and surfaces conflicts for human resolution. Spawned by /pan:review-deep. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-meta-reviewer.md))
- **pan-optimizer**: Circular optimization analyst. Reads execution trace data, identifies error/gap/redundancy patterns, and produces a structured optimization report of ranked suggestions, each naming where a person should make the fix. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-optimizer.md))
- **pan-phase-researcher**: Answers the open questions a phase's requirements leave unanswered and that block planning (at most five), each with a source and a confidence or an assumption. Writes the phase's research.md for pan-planner. Spawned by /pan:plan-phase (only when research was asked for) and /pan:plan-phase --research-only. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-phase-researcher.md))
- **pan-plan-checker**: Verifies plans will achieve phase goal before execution. Goal-backward analysis of plan quality. Spawned by /pan:plan-phase orchestrator and /pan:quick --full. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-plan-checker.md))
- **pan-planner**: Creates executable phase plans with task breakdown, dependency analysis, and goal-backward verification. Spawned by /pan:plan-phase orchestrator and /pan:quick. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-planner.md))
- **pan-previewer**: Read-only foresight agent. Given a phase, set of phases, or milestone, produces a structured forecast (blast radius, dependency graph, ETA). Spawned by /pan:preview. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-previewer.md))
- **pan-project-researcher**: Answers the open questions a project brief leaves unanswered and that block building (at most five), each with a source and a confidence or an assumption. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-project-researcher.md))
- **pan-release**: Release squad agent for the bot-army campaign. Ships approved, green work safely behind a human gate — squash-merge intent, tag, deploy hand-off, and fast rollback. Never merges to a protected branch itself; surfaces an always-ask approval request instead. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-release.md))
- **pan-review-agent**: Code review specialist — analyzes a feature branch's diff for correctness, security, performance, and requirements issues. MUST write findings to the assigned output file (.pan/review/&lt;runId&gt;/&lt;role&gt;.md). Must not modify code under review — the only permitted write is to the designated output file. ([eltmon/overdeck](https://github.com/eltmon/overdeck/blob/HEAD/sync-sources/agents/pan-review-agent.md) · ★36)
- **pan-reviewer**: Read-only code review agent. Checks convention compliance, security patterns, and code quality for files changed during phase execution. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-reviewer.md))
- **pan-roadmapper**: Creates project roadmaps with phase breakdown, requirement mapping, success criteria derivation, and coverage validation. - **pan-test-agent**: Overdeck test specialist — runs the project test suite against an agent's PR, reports failures, never edits code. ([eltmon/overdeck](https://github.com/eltmon/overdeck/blob/HEAD/sync-sources/agents/pan-test-agent.md) · ★36)
- **pan-uat-agent**: Browser-based requirement verifier — drives Playwright against the running app to check whether the issue's acceptance criteria are observable end-to-end. ([eltmon/overdeck](https://github.com/eltmon/overdeck/blob/HEAD/sync-sources/agents/pan-uat-agent.md) · ★36)
- **pan-verifier**: Verifies phase goal achievement through goal-backward analysis. Checks codebase delivers what phase promised, not just that tasks completed. Creates verification.md report. ([oharms/PanWizard](https://github.com/oharms/PanWizard/blob/HEAD/agents/pan-verifier.md))
